91:	def overlap(b1: tuple(int, int, int, int)) -> float:	(x1, y1, x2, y2) = b1	(x3, y3, x4, y4) = b2	intersection = max(0, min(x2, x4) - max(x1, x3)) * max(0, min(y2, y4) - max(y1, y3))	a1 = (x2 - x1) * (y2 - y1)	a2 = (x4 - x3) * (y4 - y3)	union = a1 + a2 - intersection	if union == 0:	return 0.0	else:	return float(intersection) / float(union)

702:	def calc_iou(box1, box2):	(x1, y1, x2, y2) = box1	(x3, y3, x4, y4) = box2	area1 = (x2 - x1) * (y2 - y1)	area2 = (x4 - x3) * (y4 - y3)	inter_area = max(min(x2, x4) - max(x1, x3), 0) * max(min(y2, y4) - max(y1, y3), 0)	union_area = area1 + area2 - inter_area	return inter_area / union_area
